# Forage Catalog

**Inventory:** 1,000 fictional products  
**Source of truth:** `catalog.json`  

This is the human-readable merchandise workbook. Product data lives outside the application so names, descriptions, variants, prices, reviews, stock, and photography can evolve without rewriting the store.
